At around 9.20 am, Brent crude futures decreased by 1.11 percent to trade at $91.72 a barrel. US West Texas Intermediate crude futures dropped by 0.97 percent, falling to $85.71 a barrel.

Negotiators from Russia and Ukraine reached an agreement on a major prisoner exchange during two days of peace talks held in Abu Dhabi. Middle East

Crude oil from northern Iraq smuggled to Iran, Afghanistan

IraqiNews · 12 Nov, 10:25 IST

Crude oil and petroleum products are smuggled from Kirkuk and Sulaymaniyah fields into Iran through Bashmaq and Parviz Khan border crossings, per an Iraqi intelligence investigation. The smuggling network transports the supplies to the Iranian border using small and medium-sized tanker trucks. Once inside Iran, the smuggled Iraqi oil is combined with Iranian petroleum products at small refineries.

The mixed gasoline is sold under false origin certificates to evade international restrictions and is re-exported mainly to Afghanistan. Iraq’s Federal Commission of Integrity opened an investigation targeting the oil smuggling network. IraqiNews ↗

Ship & Bunker · 12 Nov, 10:33 IST

Eastern Pacific Shipping completed its first truck-to-ship LNG bunkering operation in the United States. The bunkering operation was performed by the vehicle carrier Lake Victoria located in Davisville. Liquefied natural gas was delivered directly from trucks to the vessel while it was alongside.

Truck-to-ship bunkering provides flexibility by allowing liquefied natural gas supply without dedicated bunker vessels or fixed infrastructure. The recent operation builds on Eastern Pacific Shipping having conducted more than 700 liquefied natural gas bunkering operations. Eastern Pacific Shipping recently received the delivery of the world's first ammonia-fuelled very large ammonia carrier.

Kitco · 12 Nov, 01:16 IST

Oliver Blagden stated that gold producers experienced a strong second quarter in 2026 despite lower gold prices, higher expenses, and operational disruptions. Global average gold producer All-In Sustaining Costs increased by 8% quarter-on-quarter and 22% year-on-year to a record US$1,930 per ounce. Sustaining capital expenditure rose by 34% from the first quarter and 25% year over year to a record-level $441 per ounce in the second quarter.

Cash Operating Costs rose by 6% quarter-on-quarter and 18% year-on-year to US$1,215 per ounce, while Total Cash Costs increased by 4% quarter-on-quarter and 22% year-on-year to US$1,406 per ounce. Average royalty and production-tax costs fell by 11% quarter-on-quarter to US$185 per ounce, although they remained 53% higher year-on-year. The average gold price declined by 7.2% quarter-on-quarter to US$4,512 per ounce in the second quarter.

How Daily Thai Gold Price Updates Help People Understand Thailand’s Changing Gold Market

Price changes by the Gold Traders Association of Thailand are typically made in steps of 50 baht. Recent announcements for gold bars by the Gold Traders Association of Thailand have shown a gap of about 200 baht per baht weight.

One baht weight of a gold bar equals 15.244 grams while jewellery is counted at 15.16 grams. A baht weight of a gold bar holds about 14.71 grams of pure gold because most gold is 96.5% pure. According to The Nation Thailand, the Gold Traders Association of Thailand revised its prices 27 times on one volatile day in July 2026.

Moneycontrol.com, bfsi.economictimes.indiatimes.com · 11 Nov, 20:42 IST

SBI Research stated on November 11 that India's economy likely grew 7.8% in the July-September quarter, exceeding the Reserve Bank of India's projection of 7.2%. SBI Research's estimate is based on 54 high-frequency indicators spanning agriculture, industry, and services sectors. SBI Research projects real gross value added to grow between 8% and 8.2% in the second quarter.

Nominal GDP is projected by SBI Research to grow between 11% and 11.5% in the July-September quarter, up from 10.3% in the June quarter. Tractor sales dropped 18.1% in September after rising 11.9% in June, according to SBI Research. Mining output contracted by 6.2% in September, shifting from 1% growth previously.

SBI Research anticipates overall real GDP growth will moderate further to approximately 6.9% in each of the final two quarters of FY27. Moneycontrol.com ↗

SBI Research estimated India's Gross Domestic Product growth at approximately 7.8 per cent for the second quarter of the financial year 2026-27. SBI Research projected nominal Gross Domestic Product growth, which includes inflation impact, to be in the range of 11 to 11.5 per cent during the quarter.

IndiaAutos and EVs

India bans export of battery ‘black mass’ to cut import dependence for EV, storage sector

Hindustan Times Business · 11 Nov, 17:18 IST

India banned the export of lithium-ion battery black mass to retain critical minerals within the country and reduce import dependence. The Directorate General of Foreign Trade prohibited the export of lithium-ion battery black mass through a notification issued on November 10. Black mass is a powdery material obtained from shredding end-of-life lithium-ion batteries and manufacturing waste containing lithium, cobalt, nickel, and manganese.

The commerce and industry ministry spokesperson stated that the measure ensures feedstock for domestic recyclers and strengthens clean-energy supply chains. The prohibition applies to waste, scrap, and spent primary cells, batteries, and electric accumulators that are unsorted and lack lead, cadmium, or mercury. India possesses growing battery recycling and refining capacity that remains underutilized due to a lack of sufficient feedstock.

The ban complements the National Critical Mineral Mission, which includes a ₹ 1,500 crore incentive scheme approved by the Cabinet in September 2025. Hindustan Times Business ↗

Global Agriculture · 11 Nov, 21:35 IST

Ministry of Agriculture and Farmers Welfare data showed early wheat sowing slumped by over 94 percent to 19,000 hectares from 3.34 lakh hectares a year earlier. Pulses acreage dropped by 51.69 percent to 9.45 lakh hectares, with chickpea acreage decreasing by 58.91 percent to 5.90 lakh hectares. Oilseed sowing declined by 45.7 percent to 20.97 lakh hectares, driven by a 46.16 percent drop in rapeseed and mustard acreage to 20.50 lakh hectares.

Experts attributed the slow sowing start to a 13 percent deficient June to September monsoon that reduced residual soil moisture. A senior agriculture ministry official stated on November 6 that it is still too early to draw conclusions because rabi sowing continues through December. Union Agriculture Minister Shivraj Singh Chouhan chaired a review meeting and directed officials to accelerate rabi planting using residual soil moisture.

Union Agriculture Minister Shivraj Singh Chouhan advised farmers with limited irrigation access to prioritize short-duration and drought-tolerant crops over water-intensive options. Global Agriculture ↗

India gives ports 15 days to report abandoned ships and crew welfare cases

Splash247 · 12 Nov, 05:31 IST

India has ordered ports across the country to identify vessels that are abandoned, detained, or immobilised, along with related crew welfare issues. The Directorate General of Maritime Administration has given major ports, minor ports, and state maritime boards a 15-day deadline to submit this information. The directive requires authorities to report details on vessel owners, operators, managers, agents, crew counts, and welfare problems.

This reporting obligation also applies to ships that are under arrest, laid up, stranded, or unable to operate safely, regardless of formal abandonment status. Ports are required to immediately report any new cases that pose risks to life or safety. Authorities that have no qualifying vessels within their jurisdiction are still required to submit a return confirming that status. Splash247 ↗

7 stories
IndiaAutos and EVs

India bans export of battery ‘black mass’ to cut import dependence for EV, storage sector

Hindustan Times Business · 11 Nov, 17:18 IST

India banned the export of lithium-ion battery black mass to retain critical minerals within the country and reduce import dependence. The Directorate General of Foreign Trade prohibited the export of lithium-ion battery black mass through a notification issued on November 10. Black mass is a powdery material obtained from shredding end-of-life lithium-ion batteries and manufacturing waste containing lithium, cobalt, nickel, and manganese.

The commerce and industry ministry spokesperson stated that the measure ensures feedstock for domestic recyclers and strengthens clean-energy supply chains. The prohibition applies to waste, scrap, and spent primary cells, batteries, and electric accumulators that are unsorted and lack lead, cadmium, or mercury. India possesses growing battery recycling and refining capacity that remains underutilized due to a lack of sufficient feedstock.

The ban complements the National Critical Mineral Mission, which includes a ₹ 1,500 crore incentive scheme approved by the Cabinet in September 2025. Hindustan Times Business ↗
